[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision of committee --- recommendation
to alter conditions

- **[[37.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} As soon as practicable after the
  institutional head determines under subsection 37.3(2) that an
  inmate's conditions of confinement in a structured intervention unit
  should not be altered in accordance with the recommendations of a
  registered health care professional, the committee established under
  subsection 37.31(3) shall, in accordance with regulations made under
  paragraph 96(g), determine whether the inmate's conditions of
  confinement in the unit should be altered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision of committee ---
  recommendation that inmate remain in unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} As soon as practicable after the institutional head
  determines under paragraph 37.3(1)(a) that an inmate should remain in
  a structured intervention unit, the committee established under
  subsection 37.31(3) shall, in accordance with regulations made under
  paragraph 96(g), determine whether the inmate should remain in the
  unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1205948){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1205948.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision --- Commissioner

**[[37.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.4 .sectionLabel}** Thirty days after the
institutional head's determination under paragraph 37.3(1)(b) that an
inmate should remain in a structured intervention unit, the Commissioner
shall, in accordance with regulations made under paragraph 96(g),
determine whether the inmate should remain in the unit. The Commissioner
shall also make such a determination in the prescribed circumstances and
every 60 days after the Commissioner's last determination under this
section that the inmate should remain in the unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1205953){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1205953.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds

- **[[37.41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.41
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The institutional head, the
  Commissioner or the committee established under subsection 37.31(3)
  may determine that an inmate should remain in a structured
  intervention unit only if they believe on reasonable grounds that
  allowing the inmate's reintegration into the mainstream inmate
  population

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} would jeopardize the safety of the inmate or any
    other person or the security of the penitentiary; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would interfere with an investigation that could
    lead to a criminal charge or a charge under subsection 41(2) of a
    serious disciplinary offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In making the determination, the institutional head,
  the Commissioner or the committee, as the case may be, shall take into
  account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inmate's correctional plan;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the appropriateness of the inmate's confinement in
    the penitentiary;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the appropriateness of the inmate's security
    classification; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other consideration that he or she considers
    relevant.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1205966){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1205966.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of inmate's case

**[[37.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.5 .sectionLabel}** If an inmate has been
authorized to be transferred to a structured intervention unit the
prescribed number of times or in the prescribed circumstances, the
Service shall review the inmate's case in the prescribed manner and
within the prescribed period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1205970){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1205970.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of independent external
decision-maker

- **[[37.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall appoint one or
  more persons to be independent external decision-makers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To be eligible for appointment as an independent
  external decision-maker, a person must have knowledge of
  administrative decision-making processes in general. A person is not
  eligible for appointment as an independent external decision-maker if
  the person was, at any time, in the previous five years a staff member
  or appointed under subsection 6(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An independent external decision-maker is to be
  appointed for a renewable term of not more than five years and holds
  office during good behaviour, but may be removed at any time for cause
  by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Full-time or part-time

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An independent external decision-maker may be
  appointed to serve either full-time or part-time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1205982){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1205982.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration and expenses

**[[37.61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.61 .sectionLabel}** An independent
external decision-maker is to be paid

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the remuneration that is fixed by the Treasury Board;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with Treasury Board directives, any
  travel and living expenses that they incur in the performance of their
  duties and functions while absent from their ordinary place of work,
  in the case of a full-time decision-maker, and their ordinary place of
  residence, in the case of a part-time decision-maker.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1205989){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1205989.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be made available

- **[[37.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Service shall furnish to an
  independent external decision-maker all information under the
  Service's control that is relevant to the making of a determination in
  respect of an inmate by the independent external decision-maker.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to require information and
  documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of making a determination in respect
  of an inmate, an independent external decision-maker may require any
  staff member, or any person whose services are engaged by or on behalf
  of the Service,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to furnish any information that, in the
    decision-maker's opinion, the staff member or person may be able to
    furnish in relation to the inmate's case; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to produce, for examination by the decision-maker,
    any document or thing that, in the decision-maker's opinion, relates
    to the inmate's case and that may be in the possession or under the
    control of the staff member or person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of document of thing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Within 10 days after the day on which an independent
  external decision-maker makes a determination, the decision-maker
  shall return to the Service any document or thing furnished under
  subsection (1) or paragraph (2)(a) or produced under paragraph (2)(b),
  as well as any copy of one.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206000){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206000.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to inmate

- **[[37.71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.71
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before making a determination in
  respect of an inmate, an independent external decision-maker shall
  provide or cause to be provided to the inmate, in writing, in
  whichever of the two official languages of Canada is requested by the
  inmate, the information that is to be considered by the decision-maker
  or a summary of that information, other than information provided to
  the independent external decision-maker by the inmate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The independent external decision-maker may withhold
  from the inmate as much information as is strictly necessary if the
  independent external decision-maker has reasonable grounds to believe
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information should not be disclosed on the
    grounds of public interest; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the disclosure of the information would jeopardize
    the safety of any person, the security of a penitentiary or the
    conduct of any lawful investigation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206009){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206009.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written representations

**[[37.72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.72 .sectionLabel}** Before making a
determination in respect of an inmate, an independent external
decision-maker shall ensure that the inmate is given an opportunity to
make written representations to the independent external decision-maker.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206013){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206013.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to inmate

**[[37.73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.73 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
making a determination in respect of an inmate, an independent external
decision-maker may communicate with the inmate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206017){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206017.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No disclosure

- **[[37.74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.74
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), an
  independent external decision-maker shall not disclose any information
  that comes to their knowledge in the course of the exercise of their
  powers, or the performance of their duties and functions, under this
  Act or any other Act of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An independent external decision-maker may disclose
  information referred to in subsection (1) in the exercise of their
  powers or the performance of their duties and functions.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206024){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206024.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not to be witness

**[[37.75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.75 .sectionLabel}** An independent
external decision­maker is not a competent or compellable witness in any
civil proceedings in respect of any matter coming to their knowledge in
the course of the exercise or purported exercise of their powers, or the
performance or purported performance of their duties and functions,
under this Act or any other Act of Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206028){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206028.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of independent external
decision-maker

**[[37.76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.76 .sectionLabel}** No criminal or
civil proceedings lie against an independent external decision-maker for
anything done, reported or said in good faith in the course of the
exercise or purported exercise of any power, or the performance or
purported performance of any duty or function, of the independent
external decision-maker under this Act or any other Act of Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206032){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206032.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of information

**[[37.77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.77 .sectionLabel}** An independent
external decision-maker may, in accordance with regulations made under
paragraph 96(g.1), publish or otherwise disseminate information, other
than personal information, relating to any determination made by the
independent external decision-maker.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206036){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206036.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision after Commissioner's
determination

**[[37.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.8 .sectionLabel}** Thirty days after
each of the Commissioner's determinations under section 37.4 that an
inmate should remain in a structured intervention unit, an independent
external decision-maker shall, in accordance with regulations made under
paragraph 96(g.1), determine whether the inmate should remain in the
unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206040){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206040.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision after committee's determination

**[[37.81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.81 .sectionLabel}** If a committee
established under subsection 37.31(3) determines that an inmate should
remain in a structured intervention unit or determines that an inmate's
conditions of confinement in the structured intervention unit should not
be altered in accordance with a recommendation of a registered health
care professional under section 37.2, an independent external
decision-maker shall, as soon as practicable, in accordance with
regulations made under paragraph 96(g.1), determine whether the inmate
should remain in the unit or whether the inmate's conditions of
confinement in the unit should be altered.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206044){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206044.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds

- **[[37.82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.82
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The independent external
  decision-maker may determine under sections 37.8 and 37.81 that an
  inmate should remain in a structured intervention unit only if the
  independent external decision-maker believes on reasonable grounds
  that allowing the inmate's reintegration into the mainstream inmate
  population

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} would jeopardize the safety of the inmate or any
    other person or the security of the penitentiary; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would interfere with an investigation that could
    lead to a criminal charge or a charge under subsection 41(2) of a
    serious disciplinary offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In making the determination, the independent external
  decision-maker shall take into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inmate's correctional plan;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the appropriateness of the inmate's confinement in
    the penitentiary;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the appropriateness of the inmate's security
    classification; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other consideration that he or she considers
    relevant.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206057){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206057.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision --- reasonable steps

- **[[37.83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.83
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, for five consecutive days or for
  a total of 15 days during any 30-day period, an inmate confined in a
  structured intervention unit has not spent a minimum of four hours a
  day outside the inmate's cell or has not interacted, for a minimum of
  two hours a day, with others, an independent external decision-maker
  shall, as soon as practicable, determine whether the Service has taken
  all reasonable steps to provide the inmate with the opportunities
  referred to in subsection 36(1) and to encourage the inmate to avail
  themselves of those opportunities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recommendations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the independent external decision-maker determines
  that the Service has not taken all reasonable steps, he or she may
  make any recommendation to the Service that he or she considers
  appropriate to remedy the situation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to direct removal from unit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Service, within the period of seven days
  commencing on the day on which it receives recommendations, fails to
  satisfy the independent external decision-maker that it has taken all
  reasonable steps to provide the inmate with the opportunities referred
  to in subsection 36(1), the independent external decision-maker shall
  direct the Service to remove the inmate from the structured
  intervention unit and provide a notice of the direction to the
  [[Correctional Investigator]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in Part
  III.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206066){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206066.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other duties

**[[37.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.9 .sectionLabel}** An independent
external decision-maker may, in the prescribed circumstances, make a
prescribed determination or review in the prescribed manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206070){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206070.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction of movement and application
of provisions before transfer

- **[[37.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The transfer of an inmate to a
  structured intervention unit must be completed not later than five
  working days after the day on which the authorization for the transfer
  is given. Until the transfer is completed, the Service may impose
  restrictions on the inmate's movement and sections 29.01, 33, 35 to
  37.4 and 37.81 to 37.83 apply with any necessary modifications in
  respect of the inmate as though the inmate were in a structured
  intervention unit. However, the opportunity referred to in paragraph
  36(1)(b) is to be provided only if the circumstances permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation of the institutional head

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The institutional head shall, at least once every
  day, meet with the inmate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the transfer is to a
  structured intervention unit in the penitentiary where the inmate is
  confined at the time the authorization is given.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 27, s. 10](#1151516-1206079){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151516-1206079.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Discipline]{.HTitleText2} {#h-106139 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of disciplinary system

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of the
disciplinary system established by sections 40 to 44 and the regulations
is to encourage inmates to conduct themselves in a manner that promotes
the good order of the penitentiary, through a process that contributes
to the inmates' rehabilitation and successful reintegration into the
community.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}System exclusive

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** Inmates shall not be
disciplined otherwise than in accordance with sections 40 to 44 and the
regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disciplinary offences

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** An inmate commits a
disciplinary offence who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} disobeys a justifiable order of a staff member;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is, without authorization, in an area prohibited to
  inmates;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} wilfully or recklessly damages or destroys property
  that is not the inmate's;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} commits theft;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} is in possession of stolen property;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} is disrespectful toward a person in a manner that is
  likely to provoke them to be violent or toward a staff member in a
  manner that could undermine their authority or the authority of staff
  members in general;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} is abusive toward a person or intimidates them by
  threats that violence or other injury will be done to, or punishment
  inflicted on, them;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} fights with, assaults or threatens to assault another
  person;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} is in possession of, or deals in, contraband;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} without prior authorization, is in possession of, or
  deals in, an item that is not authorized by a Commissioner's Directive
  or by a written order of the institutional head;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} takes an intoxicant into the inmate's body;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} fails or refuses to provide a urine sample when
  demanded pursuant to section 54 or 55;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} creates or participates in

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a disturbance, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other activity

  that is likely to jeopardize the security of the penitentiary;

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} does anything for the purpose of escaping or
  assisting another inmate to escape;

- [(o)]{.lawlabel} offers, gives or accepts a bribe or reward;

- [(p)]{.lawlabel} without reasonable excuse, refuses to work or leaves
  work;

- [(q)]{.lawlabel} engages in gambling;

- [(r)]{.lawlabel} wilfully disobeys a written rule governing the
  conduct of inmates;

- [(r.1)]{.lawlabel} knowingly makes a false claim for compensation from
  the Crown;

- [(r.2)]{.lawlabel} throws a bodily substance towards another person;
  or

- [(s)]{.lawlabel} attempts to do, or assists another person to do,
  anything referred to in paragraphs (a) to (r).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 20, s. 40
- 2012, c. 1, s. 62
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Informal resolution

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a staff member believes on reasonable grounds that an inmate has
  committed or is committing a disciplinary offence, the staff member
  shall take all reasonable steps to resolve the matter informally,
  where possible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge may be issued

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an informal resolution is not achieved, the
  institutional head may, depending on the seriousness of the alleged
  conduct and any aggravating or mitigating factors, issue a charge of a
  minor disciplinary offence or a serious disciplinary offence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of charge

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** An inmate charged with a
disciplinary offence shall be given a written notice of the charge in
accordance with the regulations, and the notice must state whether the
charge is minor or serious.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearing

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  charge of a disciplinary offence shall be dealt with in accordance
  with the prescribed procedure, including a hearing conducted in the
  prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presence of inmate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A hearing mentioned in subsection (1) shall be
  conducted with the inmate present unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inmate is voluntarily absent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person conducting the hearing believes on
    reasonable grounds that the inmate's presence would jeopardize the
    safety of any person present at the hearing; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the inmate seriously disrupts the hearing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The person conducting the hearing shall not find the
  inmate guilty unless satisfied beyond a reasonable doubt, based on the
  evidence presented at the hearing, that the inmate committed the
  disciplinary offence in question.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disciplinary sanctions

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inmate who is found guilty of a disciplinary offence is liable, in
  accordance with the regulations made under paragraphs 96(i) and (j),
  to one or more of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a warning or reprimand;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a loss of privileges;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an order to make restitution, including in respect
    of any property that is damaged or destroyed as a result of the
    offence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a fine; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} performance of extra duties.

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2019, c. 27, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collection of fine or restitution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fine or restitution imposed pursuant to
  subsection (1) may be collected in the prescribed manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 20, s. 44
- 2012, c. 1, s. 63
- [2019, c. 27, s. 11](#1151520-1206098){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151520-1206098.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20120613.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Summary Conviction Offences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-106202 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary conviction offences

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** Every person commits a
summary conviction offence who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is in possession of contraband beyond the visitor
  control point in a penitentiary;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is in possession of anything referred to in
  paragraph (b) or (c) of the definition "contraband" in section 2
  before the visitor control point at a penitentiary;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} delivers contraband to, or receives contraband from,
  an inmate;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} without prior authorization, delivers jewellery to,
  or receives jewellery from, an inmate; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} trespasses at a penitentiary.

### [Search and Seizure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-106210 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-106211 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** In sections 47 to 67,

[[body cavity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[body cavity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rectum or
    vagina; ([cavité corporelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[body cavity search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[body cavity search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the physical
    probing of a body cavity, in the prescribed manner; ([examen des
    cavités corporelles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[body scan search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[body scan search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a search of a body by
    means of a prescribed body scanner that is conducted in the
    prescribed manner. ([fouille par balayage corporel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[frisk search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[frisk search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a manual search, or a search by technical means,
      of the clothed body, in the prescribed manner, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a search of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} personal possessions, including clothing, that
        the person may be carrying, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any coat or jacket that the person has been
        requested to remove,

      in accordance with any applicable regulations made under paragraph
      96(l); ([fouille par palpation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[non-intrusive search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-intrusive search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a search of a non-intrusive nature of the clothed
      body by technical means, in the prescribed manner, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a search of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} personal possessions, including clothing, that
        the person may be carrying, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any coat or jacket that the person has been
        requested to remove,

      in accordance with any applicable regulations made under paragraph
      96(l); ([fouille discrète]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[strip search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[strip search]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a visual inspection of the naked body, in the
      prescribed manner, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a search, in accordance with any applicable
      regulations made under paragraph 96(l), of all clothing, things in
      the clothing, and other personal possessions that the person may
      be carrying; ([fouille à nu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[urinalysis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[urinalysis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a prescribed procedure by
    which a person provides a urine sample, by the normal excretory
    process, for analysis. ([prise d'échantillon
    d'urine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 20, s. 46
- 1995, c. 42, s. 13(F)
- [2019, c. 27, s. 12](#1151523-1488854){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1151523-1488854.html"
  role="button"}
:::
